Avera testing report

Overview

Avera repository security, limits and reliability assessment, executed November 01, 2026, 01:50 PM EST. Revision 6729017694c87345cdc0b52e645d77867f613808. The original checkout was clean. Tests ran against local code, in-memory SDK/provider fixtures and a local Chromium fixture build. No production data was changed and no hosted app was deployed.

405
GREEN · successful tests
4
RED · failed tests
4
ORANGE · remediation actions for failures
8
Not tested

These results do not certify production security, availability, live AI accuracy, deployed row-level security, real Plaid Link, private storage or durable cloud persistence. Not-tested cases are excluded from pass/fail totals. Risk describes the observed failure: Critical = severe unauthorized exposure/control or major financial-integrity impact; Moderate = meaningful security/reliability weakness; Minor = limited test or usability issue. No Critical finding was demonstrated; untested controls remain unknown.

Each existing Vitest assertion case is recorded separately (399 baseline cases), plus four new adversarial cases, four quality gates and two browser runner cases. Browser checkpoints are nested evidence, not additional pass totals. Diagnostic reruns are not counted twice. For non-chat tests, “Prompt / input” gives the actual runner command and executable fixture; it is not a claim that a natural-language prompt was submitted to an AI. Backend handlers were invoked directly with fake SDK/providers. The new adversarial expectations are audit criteria based on the repository’s shared request guard; 16 KB is the proposed limit for these small JSON endpoints, not a verified hosted gateway limit.

Failed tests

CaseFailureRisk
TC-117edits and deletes logged spending through the budget UIMinor
TC-400AD-01 feedback rejects 100KB ignored padding before persistenceModerate
TC-401AD-02 contribution rejects 100KB ignored padding before persistenceModerate
TC-409Exercises the Ask Avera page and drawerMinor

Remediations

TC-117 · Minor: Freeze this test’s clock or explicitly select the October budget period before opening transactions. Add a month-rollover regression and rerun the unmodified complete suite after repairing the fixture.

TC-400 · Moderate: Use readJsonLimited(req) before field validation (for example a 16,384-byte limit), return requestFailureResponse(error), and add oversized/chunked/stalled-body regressions. Apply the same guard consistently to the other req.json() handlers. Verify deployed gateway size/time limits; consider per-user write limits.

TC-401 · Moderate: Use readJsonLimited(req) before field validation (for example a 16,384-byte limit), return requestFailureResponse(error), and add oversized/chunked/stalled-body regressions. Apply the same guard consistently to the other req.json() handlers. Verify deployed gateway size/time limits; consider per-user write limits.

TC-409 · Minor: Keep the composer within the available viewport above mobile navigation. Reduce or collapse the tall header at short heights, allow appropriate content scrolling, and account for keyboard/visualViewport height. Rerun the full page/drawer viewport/theme matrix and test a real mobile keyboard.

TC-012 — legacy migration returns a persisted vault ID needed by first sync/disconnect

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: legacy migration returns a persisted vault ID needed by first sync/disconnect.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/acceptance-legacy-plaid.test.js -t 'legacy\ migration\ returns\ a\ persisted\ vault\ ID\ needed\ by\ first\ sync/disconnect'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/acceptance-legacy-plaid.test.js:4

Exact executable test fixture
it('legacy migration returns a persisted vault ID needed by first sync/disconnect',async()=>{
 const rows=[];
 const item={id:'legacy-item',item_id:'sandbox-item',created_by_id:'fixture-user',sync_cursor:'old-cursor',demo_profile:'early_career'};
 const vault={filter:vi.fn(async()=>({items:rows,has_more:false})),upsert:vi.fn(async(values)=>{rows.push(...values.map(value=>({...value,id:'persisted-vault-id'})));})};
 const client={asServiceRole:{entities:{PlaidCredential:vault,PlaidItem:{filter:vi.fn(async()=>({items:[{...item,access_token:'access-sandbox-fixture-only'}],has_more:false})),update:vi.fn(async()=>{})}}}};
 const credential=await credentialFor(client,{id:'fixture-user'},item);
 expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
 expect(credential.sync_cursor).toBe('');
 expect(client.asServiceRole.entities.PlaidItem.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(item.id,{access_token:''});
 // plaidSync.update(credential.id, ...) and plaidDisconnect.delete(credential.id)
 // need the saved row ID. An unsaved payload cannot fulfill that contract.
 expect(credential.id).toBe('persisted-vault-id');
})

TC-018 — a late name update cannot restore a logged-out account

Evidence / fixture source: tests/account-name.test.jsx:39

Exact executable test fixture
it('a late name update cannot restore a logged-out account', async () => {
  let complete;sdk.auth.updateMe.mockReturnValue(new Promise(resolve=>{complete=resolve;}));
  render(<AuthProvider><Account/></AuthProvider>);const input=await screen.findByLabelText('Name');
  fireEvent.change(input,{target:{value:'New name'}});fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Save name'}));
  await waitFor(()=>expect(sdk.auth.updateMe).toHaveBeenCalled());fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Sign out'}));
  await act(async()=>complete({id:'a',display_name:'New name'}));
  expect(screen.getByText('Signed out')).toBeTruthy();expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Name')).toBeNull();
})

  const gross=insight({incomes:[{amount:3000,income_basis:'gross'}]});expect(gross.income_basis).toBe('gross');expect(gross.warnings.join(' ')).toContain('Never call');
  expect(insight({incomes:[{amount:100,income_basis:'net'},{amount:100,income_basis:'gross'}]}).income_basis).toBe('mixed_or_unknown');
 })

TC-095 — signs out at 15 minutes and cancels its timer on unmount

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: signs out at 15 minutes and cancels its timer on unmount.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/idle-logout.test.jsx -t 'signs\ out\ at\ 15\ minutes\ and\ cancels\ its\ timer\ on\ unmount'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/idle-logout.test.jsx:7

Exact executable test fixture
it('signs out at 15 minutes and cancels its timer on unmount', () => {
  const logout = vi.fn();
  const view = renderHook(() => useIdleLogout('a', logout));
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS - 1));
  expect(logout).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(1));
  expect(logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  view.unmount();
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS));
  expect(logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
})

TC-096 — keyboard activity extends the deadline, but focus does not

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: keyboard activity extends the deadline, but focus does not.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/idle-logout.test.jsx -t 'keyboard\ activity\ extends\ the\ deadline,\ but\ focus\ does\ not'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/idle-logout.test.jsx:18

Exact executable test fixture
it('keyboard activity extends the deadline, but focus does not', () => {
  const logout = vi.fn();
  renderHook(() => useIdleLogout('a', logout));
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(10 * 60 * 1000));
  act(() => window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown')));
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(10 * 60 * 1000));
  act(() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event('focus')));
  expect(logout).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(5 * 60 * 1000));
  expect(logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
})

TC-097 — expires an old session after reload and on returning from a suspended tab

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: expires an old session after reload and on returning from a suspended tab.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/idle-logout.test.jsx -t 'expires\ an\ old\ session\ after\ reload\ and\ on\ returning\ from\ a\ suspended\ tab'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/idle-logout.test.jsx:29

Exact executable test fixture
it('expires an old session after reload and on returning from a suspended tab', () => {
  const logout = vi.fn();
  localStorage.setItem(activityKey('a'), String(Date.now() - IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS));
  const view = renderHook(() => useIdleLogout('a', logout));
  expect(logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  view.unmount(); localStorage.clear(); logout.mockClear();
  renderHook(() => useIdleLogout('a', logout));
  vi.setSystemTime(Date.now() + IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS);
  act(() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event('focus')));
  expect(logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
})

Evidence / fixture source: tests/idle-logout.test.jsx:40

Exact executable test fixture
it('shares activity and sign-out across tabs', () => {
  const logout = vi.fn();
  renderHook(() => useIdleLogout('a', logout));
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(10 * 60 * 1000));
  act(() => window.dispatchEvent(new StorageEvent('storage', {key: activityKey('a'), newValue: String(Date.now())})));
  act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTime(10 * 60 * 1000));
  expect(logout).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  act(() => window.dispatchEvent(new StorageEvent('storage', {key: activityKey('a'), newValue: null})));
  expect(logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
})

Exact executable test fixture
it('every financial schema requires owner-only RLS including admins', () => {
    const dir=process.cwd()+'/base44/entities/';
    for(const file of readdirSync(dir).filter(x=>!['User.jsonc','PlaidCredential.jsonc','ServiceUsage.jsonc'].includes(x))) {
      const schema=JSON.parse(readFileSync(dir+file,'utf8'));
      // Create may anchor ownership on either accepted form; both restrict to the signed-in owner.
      expect(
        [{user_condition:{id:'{{user.id}}'}}, {created_by_id:'{{user.id}}'}].some(r => JSON.stringify(r) === JSON.stringify(schema.rls.create)),
        `${file}:create must be owner-anchored`
      ).toBe(true);
      for(const op of ['read','update','delete']) {
        expect(schema.rls[op],`${file}:${op} must be owner-only, including admins`).toEqual({created_by_id:'{{user.id}}'});
      }
    }
  })

npx vitest run tests/integration.test.jsx -t 'concurrent\ goal\ contributions\ and\ retries\ retain\ both\ independent\ updates'

Evidence / fixture source: tests/integration.test.jsx:207

Exact executable test fixture
it('concurrent goal contributions and retries retain both independent updates', async () => {
  const goal=await data.createRecord('goal',{name:'Savings',target_amount:1000,saved_amount:100});
  await Promise.all([
    data.logGoalContribution(goal.id,10,'request-first-0001'),
    data.logGoalContribution(goal.id,20,'request-second-0002'),
  ]);
  await data.logGoalContribution(goal.id,10,'request-first-0001');
  expect((await data.listRecords('goal'))[0].saved_amount).toBe(130);
  expect(rows.GoalContribution).toHaveLength(2);
  expect(rows.Goal[0].saved_amount).toBe(100);
  await data.updateRecord('goal',goal.id,{name:'Updated',target_amount:1500,saved_amount:100});
  expect((await data.listRecords('goal'))[0].saved_amount).toBe(130);
  await data.deleteRecord('goal',goal.id);
  expect(rows.GoalContribution).toHaveLength(0);
})

TC-117 — edits and deletes logged spending through the budget UI

RED · FAIL · Risk: Minor

Summary: Verifies: edits and deletes logged spending through the budget UI.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/integration.test.jsx -t 'edits\ and\ deletes\ logged\ spending\ through\ the\ budget\ UI'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: The UI test could not find “Edit spending Lunch”. Repeated at the current date: failed again. Diagnostic with the clock fixed to 2026-10-15: passed. The fixture uses 2026-10-01 but the UI defaults to the current month (November). This demonstrates a date-dependent test fixture, not a confirmed spending-edit product defect.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/integration.test.jsx:223

Exact executable test fixture
it('edits and deletes logged spending through the budget UI', async () => {
  await data.createRecord('budgetCategory',{name:'Food',monthly_limit:100});
  await data.createRecord('transaction',{description:'Lunch',category:'Food',amount:10,type:'expense',date:'2026-10-01'});
  render(<BillsBudget />);
  const tab=await screen.findByRole('tab',{name:'Budget',exact:true});
  fireEvent.mouseDown(tab,{button:0,ctrlKey:false});fireEvent.click(tab);
  fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button',{name:/View transactions/}));
  fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button',{name:'Edit spending Lunch'}));
  fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Amount'),{target:{value:'15'}});
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Save changes'}));
  await waitFor(()=>expect(rows.Transaction[0].amount).toBe(15));
  await waitFor(()=>expect(screen.queryByRole('button',{name:'Save changes'})).toBeNull());
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Delete spending Lunch'}));
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Delete',exact:true}));
  await waitFor(()=>expect(rows.Transaction).toHaveLength(0));
})

Suggested remediation: Freeze this test’s clock or explicitly select the October budget period before opening transactions. Add a month-rollover regression and rerun the unmodified complete suite after repairing the fixture.

TC-126 — recategorization moves spending between budget cards and persists after reload

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: recategorization moves spending between budget cards and persists after reload.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/integration.test.jsx -t 'recategorization\ moves\ spending\ between\ budget\ cards\ and\ persists\ after\ reload'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/integration.test.jsx:350

Exact executable test fixture
it('recategorization moves spending between budget cards and persists after reload', async () => {
  const month = new Date();
  const date = `${month.getFullYear()}-${String(month.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')}-05`;
  await data.createRecord('budgetCategory', {name:'Food',monthly_limit:100});
  await data.createRecord('budgetCategory', {name:'Travel',monthly_limit:200});
  const transaction = await data.createRecord('transaction', {description:'Lunch',category:'Food',amount:25,type:'expense',date});
  await data.assignTransactionCategory(transaction.id, ' Travel ');
  let view = render(<BillsBudget />);
  await screen.findByRole('tab', {name:'Budget'});
  fireEvent.mouseDown(screen.getByRole('tab', {name:'Budget'}), {button:0,ctrlKey:false});
  await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole('heading', {name:'Travel'})).toBeTruthy());
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', {name:'Travel'}).closest('.rounded-2xl').textContent).toContain('$25.00');
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', {name:'Food'}).closest('.rounded-2xl').textContent).toContain('$0.00');
  view.unmount();
  await data.assignTransactionCategory(transaction.id, ' Hobbies ');
  expect(rows.BudgetCategory.find(row => row.name === 'Hobbies').monthly_limit).toBe(0);
  expect(rows.Transaction[0].category).toBe('Hobbies');
  await data.assignTransactionCategory(transaction.id, 'hobbies');
  expect(rows.BudgetCategory.filter(row => row.name === 'Hobbies')).toHaveLength(1);
  view = render(<BillsBudget />);
  await screen.findByRole('tab', {name:'Budget'});
  fireEvent.mouseDown(screen.getByRole('tab', {name:'Budget'}), {button:0,ctrlKey:false});
  await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByRole('heading', {name:'Hobbies'})).toBeTruthy());
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', {name:'Hobbies'}).closest('.rounded-2xl').textContent).toContain('$25.00');
})

Evidence / fixture source: tests/linkedin-source.test.js:41

Exact executable test fixture
it('caps selected search jobs and detail IDs at twelve while preserving search order', async () => {
    const jobs=Array.from({length:20},(_,i)=>job(String(i+1)));
    fetchMock.mockResolvedValueOnce(Response.json({status:'OK',data:jobs}))
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(Response.json({status:'OK',data:[...jobs].reverse()}));
    const results=await searchLinkedIn(profile);
    const ids=new URL(fetchMock.mock.calls[1][0]).searchParams.get('job_id').split(',');
    expect(ids).toEqual(jobs.slice(0,12).map(row=>row.job_id));
    expect(results).toHaveLength(12);
    expect(results.map(row=>row.url)).toEqual(jobs.slice(0,12).map(row=>row.job_apply_link));
    expect(fetchMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  })

Exact executable test fixture
it('groups linked estimates by assigned transaction categories without duplicate imports', () => {
    const transactions = ['2026-08-01', '2026-09-01'].flatMap((date, index) => [
      { date, amount: 300, type: 'expense', category: 'Food', plaid_item_id: 'item', plaid_transaction_id: `food-${index}` },
      { date, amount: 100, type: 'expense', category: '', plaid_item_id: 'item', plaid_transaction_id: `other-${index}` },

    const cancel=vi.fn();

    expect(cancel).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
  })

TC-221 — bounded request parsing preserves UTF-8 data across streaming chunk boundaries

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: preserves UTF-8 data across streaming chunk boundaries.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/request-safety.test.js -t 'bounded\ request\ parsing\ preserves\ UTF\-8\ data\ across\ streaming\ chunk\ boundaries'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/request-safety.test.js:51

Exact executable test fixture
it('preserves UTF-8 data across streaming chunk boundaries', async () => {
    const bytes=new TextEncoder().encode('{"name":"é"}');
    const req=new Request('https://fixture.test',{method:'POST',body:new ReadableStream({start(c){for(const b of bytes)c.enqueue(new Uint8Array([b]));c.close();}}),duplex:'half'});
    expect(await readJsonLimited(req)).toEqual({name:'é'});
  })

Evidence / fixture source: tests/request-safety.test.js:82

Exact executable test fixture
it('enforces minute limits across concurrent calls without resetting counters', async () => {
    const {client,rows}=quotaClient();
    const results=await Promise.allSettled(Array.from({length:10},()=>claimUsage(client,{id:'a'},'askAvera',0)));
    expect(results.filter(r=>r.status==='fulfilled')).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(results.filter(r=>r.status==='rejected').every(r=>r.reason.status===429)).toBe(true);
    expect([...rows.values()].find(r=>r.usage_key==='a:askAvera:60000:0').used).toBe(6);
    await expect(claimUsage(client,{id:'b'},'askAvera',0)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  })

      match_breakdown:{skills:30,interests:25,experience:20,preferences:5},
    })),related_roles_searched:true}});
    render(<OpportunityResults profile={{skills:'Bookkeeping'}} />);
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button',{name:'Find opportunities'}));

    expect(screen.getAllByText(/8\/10 profile match/)).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(screen.queryByText('Matched role 7')).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.getAllByText(/Required skills not listed in your profile: Payroll/)).toHaveLength(6);

TC-294 — critical function invocation boundaries (mocked, not backend authorization) sends successful exchanges with follow-ups and clears them when the dataset changes

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: sends successful exchanges with follow-ups and clears them when the dataset changes.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/stage2.test.jsx -t 'critical\ function\ invocation\ boundaries\ \(mocked,\ not\ backend\ authorization\)\ sends\ successful\ exchanges\ with\ follow\-ups\ and\ clears\ them\ when\ the\ dataset\ changes'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/stage2.test.jsx:168

Exact executable test fixture
it('sends successful exchanges with follow-ups and clears them when the dataset changes', async () => {
    setModeOwner('a'); setDataMode('manual');
    sdk.functions.invoke.mockResolvedValue({data:{answer:'Your recorded planning surplus is $500.'}});
    render(<AskAveraConversation />);
    const send = async question => {
      fireEvent.change(screen.getByLabelText('Ask Avera'), {target:{value:question}});
      fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', {name:'Send question'}));
      await waitFor(() => expect(screen.getByLabelText('Ask Avera').disabled).toBe(false));
    };
    await send('What is my surplus?');
    await send('What does that mean?');
    expect(sdk.functions.invoke.mock.calls[1][1].history).toEqual([
      {role:'user',text:'What is my surplus?'},
      {role:'assistant',text:'Your recorded planning surplus is $500.'},
    ]);
    sdk.functions.invoke.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('AI unavailable'));
    await send('Failed question');
    await send('Try another question');
    expect(sdk.functions.invoke.mock.calls.at(-1)[1].history.some(m => m.text === 'Failed question')).toBe(false);
    await send('Another follow-up');
    await send('One more follow-up');
    expect(sdk.functions.invoke.mock.calls.at(-1)[1].history).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(sdk.functions.invoke.mock.calls.at(-1)[1].history.some(m => m.text === 'What is my surplus?')).toBe(false);
    act(() => setDataMode('sandbox'));
    expect(screen.queryByText('What is my surplus?')).toBeNull();
    await send('New dataset question');
    expect(sdk.functions.invoke.mock.calls.at(-1)[1]).not.toHaveProperty('history');
  })

it('does not log upstream exception contents during Plaid sync failure',async()=>{
 const spy=vi.spyOn(console,'error').mockImplementation(()=>{});
 try {
  fetchMock.mockRejectedValue(new Error('synthetic-private-marker'));
  await sync(req());
  expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Sandbox sync failed',{code:'SYNC_FAILED'});
  expect(JSON.stringify(spy.mock.calls)).not.toContain('synthetic-private-marker');
 } finally {spy.mockRestore();}
})

TC-349 — demo and UI fixtures, distinct from real Plaid Sandbox early_career generates labeled finite records and a valid Sandbox configuration

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: early_career generates labeled finite records and a valid Sandbox configuration.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run tests/stage3-demo.test.jsx -t 'demo\ and\ UI\ fixtures,\ distinct\ from\ real\ Plaid\ Sandbox\ early_career\ generates\ labeled\ finite\ records\ and\ a\ valid\ Sandbox\ configuration'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: tests/stage3-demo.test.jsx:34

Exact executable test fixture
it.each(DEMO_PROFILE_KEYS)('%s generates labeled finite records and a valid Sandbox configuration',async profile=>{
    const today=new Date(2026,9,28);const dataset=buildDemoDataset(profile,today);const cfg=demoSandboxConfig(profile,today);
    for(const values of Object.values(dataset).filter(Array.isArray))for(const row of values){expect(row.source).toBe('Sample data');expect(row.demo_profile).toBe(profile);if('amount'in row)expect(Number.isFinite(row.amount)&&row.amount>=0).toBe(true);}
    expect(dataset.budgetCategory.some(c=>dataset.bill.some(b=>b.category===c.name))).toBe(false);
    expect(dataset.transaction.every(t=>t.date<='2026-10-28')).toBe(true);
    const created=await createDemoRecords(dataset,async(key,values)=>Promise.all(values.map(v=>sdk.entities[data.DATA_ENTITIES[key]].create(v))),async()=>true);
    for(const mortgage of rows.MortgageRecord)expect(created.property.some(p=>p.id===mortgage.property_id)).toBe(true);
    expect(cfg.institution_id).toBeTruthy();expect(cfg.products).toEqual(['transactions']);
    expect(buildDemoDataset(profile,today,{includeTransactionData:false}).transaction).toEqual([]);
    expect(rows.HouseholdProfile).toHaveLength(1);
  })

TC-400 — AD-01 feedback rejects 100KB ignored padding before persistence

RED · FAIL · Risk: Moderate

Summary: Verifies: AD-01 feedback rejects 100KB ignored padding before persistence.

Prompt / input:
npx vitest run work/platform-testing/adversarial.test.js -t 'AD\-01\ feedback\ rejects\ 100KB\ ignored\ padding\ before\ persistence'
Input: {rating:5,message:"Synthetic test feedback",consent:true,request_id:"audit-local-request-0001",padding:"x".repeat(100000)}

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass. Reject oversized request with HTTP 413 before persistence.

Observed outcome: Expected HTTP 413. Actual HTTP 200; one mocked persistence write occurred after parsing a valid payload with 100,000 extra padding characters. No hosted requests or real data writes occurred.

Evidence / fixture source: work/platform-testing/adversarial.test.js:18

Exact executable test fixture
it('AD-01 feedback rejects 100KB ignored padding before persistence', async () => {
  const response = await feedback(req({ rating: 5, message: 'Synthetic test feedback', consent: true, request_id: 'audit-local-request-0001', padding: 'x'.repeat(100000) }));
  console.log('AD-01 observed', response.status, 'writes', fixture.client.entities.Feedback.upsert.mock.calls.length);
  expect(response.status).toBe(413);
  expect(fixture.client.entities.Feedback.upsert).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
})

Suggested remediation: Use readJsonLimited(req) before field validation (for example a 16,384-byte limit), return requestFailureResponse(error), and add oversized/chunked/stalled-body regressions. Apply the same guard consistently to the other req.json() handlers. Verify deployed gateway size/time limits; consider per-user write limits.

it('AD-02 contribution rejects 100KB ignored padding before persistence', async () => {
  const response = await contribute(req({ goal_id: 'goal-local', amount: 10, request_id: 'audit-local-request-0002', padding: 'x'.repeat(100000) }));

TC-402 — AD-03 JSON parser accepts exactly its byte limit and rejects one byte above

GREEN · PASS · Risk: —

Summary: Verifies: AD-03 JSON parser accepts exactly its byte limit and rejects one byte above.

npx vitest run work/platform-testing/adversarial.test.js -t 'AD\-03\ JSON\ parser\ accepts\ exactly\ its\ byte\ limit\ and\ rejects\ one\ byte\ above'

Expected: All assertions in the linked fixture must pass.

Observed outcome: Passed every assertion in this case against the local implementation and its configured fixtures.

Evidence / fixture source: work/platform-testing/adversarial.test.js:30

Exact executable test fixture
it('AD-03 JSON parser accepts exactly its byte limit and rejects one byte above', async () => {
  const body = JSON.stringify({ x: 'a'.repeat(12) });
  expect(new TextEncoder().encode(body).length).toBe(20);
  expect(await readJsonLimited(new Request('https://fixture.test', { method: 'POST', body }), 20)).toEqual({ x: 'a'.repeat(12) });
  await expect(readJsonLimited(new Request('https://fixture.test', { method: 'POST', body: body + ' ' }), 20)).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 413 });
})

Evidence and reproduction

See the accompanying evidence ZIP for JSON results, browser logs, fixture source excerpts, diagnostic reruns and new adversarial test source. Run commands from the repository root with installed lockfile dependencies. To run the new tests, restore their original work/platform-testing paths from evidence/source/work/platform-testing. No application code was repaired during this assessment.

Browser setup: build with VITE_BASE44_APP_ID=fixture VITE_BASE44_APP_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:4173 npm run build, then npm run preview -- --host 127.0.0.1 --port 4173. Both runners intercept API calls and block external HTTPS assets. Earlier dev-server/port attempts were inconclusive setup attempts and are excluded; final recorded browser outcomes use the fixture build.
